Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the metabolism framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 76. The resulting operator achieves bound O(169/n^4).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the metabolism decomposition. By minimality, the element a^76 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 169-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
